# REV-4 test-by-test inventory

Historical iteration 01/02 inventory. The current numeric review is [TEST_SCORES.md](TEST_SCORES.md), explained in [SCORE_REVIEW.md](SCORE_REVIEW.md).

Baseline `3481919832272d3a545f8d7a1c6c98a1f678fc58`. Companion to [REVIEW.md](REVIEW.md). One row per baseline declaration, including parameterized declarations. Iteration 02 corrects the original Go inventory omission: 511 Go plus 742 TypeScript declarations, 1,253 total. The 50 REMOVED rows identify iteration 02 deletions; line numbers remain historical. Remaining suite: 505 Go plus 698 TypeScript declarations, 1,203 total. KEEP is a conservative screening disposition, not proof of minimality. Evidence is an observed assertion or named subcase; it is not a mutation result. Source line numbers refer to the baseline.

| 15 | `TestReleaseStable_SemVerRegexValidation` | REMOVED | Compiles a regex copied into this test; never executes the workflow validator. Editing the real release validation can leave all cases green. Retain: Retain workflow integrity and Makefile missing-input checks as limited wiring checks, plus actual release CI. Those are not full end-to-end release validation. Deleting this case loses no executed production release behavior; release success-path coverage remains a known gap. |

Retain: Retain workflow integrity and Makefile missing-input checks as limited wiring checks, plus actual release CI. Those are not full end-to-end release validation. Deleting this case loses no executed production release behavior; release success-path coverage remains a known gap. |

Deleting this case loses no executed production release behavior; release success-path coverage remains a known gap. |
